ok so this is the schema -- =========================================================
-- Solob Wrapper V1.1 ? Canonical SQLite Schema
-- Purpose: Anchor-referential, audit-first memory spine
-- Database role: Index + provenance ledger (NOT source of truth)
-- =========================================================

PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON;

-- =========================================================
-- TABLE: anchors
-- Indexes external, read-only anchor sources (PDF, JSON, text)
-- =========================================================
CREATE TABLE anchors (
    anchor_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    anchor_type TEXT NOT NULL,          -- lexicon | book | letter | other
    title TEXT NOT NULL,
    source_path TEXT NOT NULL,           -- filesystem path to anchor
    source_format TEXT NOT NULL,         -- json | pdf | txt
    status TEXT NOT NULL,                -- canon | working
    provenance TEXT NOT NULL,            -- human-entered source description
    import_session_id TEXT NOT NULL,     -- ingestion batch identifier
    created_at TEXT NOT NULL             -- ISO-8601 timestamp
);

-- =========================================================
-- TABLE: chunks
-- Atomic, referenceable statements extracted from anchors
-- =========================================================
CREATE TABLE chunks (
    chunk_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    anchor_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    anchor_locator TEXT NOT NULL,        -- page, section, lexicon row, etc.
    lexicon_word TEXT,                   -- headword if lexicon-derived (NULL otherwise)
    content TEXT NOT NULL,               -- exact extracted text (no mutation)

    truth_type TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (
        truth_type IN (
            'definition',
            'event',
            'empirical',
            'interpretive',
            'causal',
            'relational'
        )
    ),

    mutation_mode TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (
        mutation_mode IN (
            'append-only',
            'versioned',
            'time-bounded',
            're-index-only'
        )
    ),

    confidence REAL CHECK (
        confidence IS NULL
        OR (confidence >= 0 AND confidence <= 1)
    ),

    import_session_id TEXT NOT NULL,     -- ingestion batch identifier
    created_at TEXT NOT NULL,

    FOREIGN KEY (anchor_id)
        REFERENCES anchors(anchor_id)
        ON DELETE RESTRICT
);

-- =========================================================
-- TABLE: provenance_notes
-- Non-mutative annotations and corrections
-- =========================================================
CREATE TABLE provenance_notes (
    note_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    target_type TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (target_type IN ('anchor', 'chunk')),
    target_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    note TEXT NOT NULL,
    author TEXT NOT NULL,
    created_at TEXT NOT NULL
);


    CHECK (
        (target_type = 'anchor' AND target_id IN (SELECT anchor_id FROM anchors))
        OR
        (target_type = 'chunk' AND target_id IN (SELECT chunk_id FROM chunks))
    )
);

-- =========================================================
-- TABLE: runs
-- Logged system queries and outputs (audit trail)
-- =========================================================
CREATE TABLE runs (
    run_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    input_query TEXT NOT NULL,
    output_text TEXT NOT NULL,
    created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
    verdict TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (
        verdict IN ('ok', 'unknown', 'flagged')
    )
);

-- =========================================================
-- TABLE: run_citations
-- Normalized citation graph between runs and chunks
-- =========================================================
CREATE TABLE run_citations (
    run_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    chunk_id TEXT NOT NULL,

    PRIMARY KEY (run_id, chunk_id),

    FOREIGN KEY (run_id)
        REFERENCES runs(run_id)
        ON DELETE CASCADE,

    FOREIGN KEY (chunk_id)
        REFERENCES chunks(chunk_id)
        ON DELETE RESTRICT
);

-- END OF SCHEMA ? V1.1

import sqlite3
from pathlib import Path

# -----------------------------
# PATHS
# -----------------------------

BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
DB_PATH = BASE_DIR / "data" / "memory.db"

# -----------------------------
# SANITY CHECKS
# -----------------------------

def sanity_check():
    if not DB_PATH.exists():
        raise FileNotFoundError("[ERROR] memory.db does not exist. Run init_db.py first.")

    conn = sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH)
    cursor = conn.cursor()

    try:
        print("? Running sanity checks...\n")

        # 1. Check required tables exist
        required_tables = {
            "anchors",
            "chunks",
            "provenance_notes",
            "runs",
        }

        cursor.execute(
            "SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table';"
        )
        existing_tables = {row[0] for row in cursor.fetchall()}

        missing_tables = required_tables - existing_tables
        if missing_tables:
            raise RuntimeError(f"[ERROR] Missing tables: {missing_tables}")

        print("[OK] All required tables present.")

        # 2. Anchors must exist before chunks
        cursor.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM anchors;")
        anchor_count = cursor.fetchone()[0]

        cursor.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM chunks;")
        chunk_count = cursor.fetchone()[0]

        if chunk_count > 0 and anchor_count == 0:
            raise RuntimeError("[ERROR] Chunks exist but no anchors registered.")

        print(f"[OK] Anchors: {anchor_count}, Chunks: {chunk_count}")

        # 3. No NULL import_session_id in anchors or chunks
        cursor.execute(
            "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM anchors WHERE import_session_id IS NULL;"
        )
        bad_anchors = cursor.fetchone()[0]

        cursor.execute(
            "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM chunks WHERE import_session_id IS NULL;"
        )
        bad_chunks = cursor.fetchone()[0]

        if bad_anchors > 0 or bad_chunks > 0:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"[ERROR] Missing import_session_id "
                f"(anchors: {bad_anchors}, chunks: {bad_chunks})"
            )

        print("[OK] import_session_id present on all anchors and chunks.")

        # 4. Lexicon chunks must have lexicon_word
        cursor.execute(
            """
            SELECT COUNT(*) FROM chunks
            WHERE truth_type = 'definition'
              AND lexicon_word IS NULL;
            """
        )
        bad_lexicon = cursor.fetchone()[0]

        if bad_lexicon > 0:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"[ERROR] {bad_lexicon} lexicon definition chunks missing lexicon_word."
            )

        print("[OK] Lexicon chunks correctly populated.")

        # 5. Chunk IDs must be unique (primary key sanity)
        cursor.execute(
            """
            SELECT COUNT(*) FROM (
                SELECT chunk_id FROM chunks
                GROUP BY chunk_id
                HAVING COUNT(*) > 1
            );
            """
        )
        duplicate_chunks = cursor.fetchone()[0]

        if duplicate_chunks > 0:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"[ERROR] Duplicate chunk_id values detected: {duplicate_chunks}"
            )

        print("[OK] No duplicate chunk IDs detected.")

        print("\n? SANITY CHECK PASSED ? DATABASE IS COHERENT.")

    finally:
        conn.close()


# -----------------------------
# SCRIPT ENTRY POINT
# -----------------------------

if __name__ == "__main__":
    sanity_check()

# scripts/sanity_check.py
from __future__ import annotations

import sqlite3
from pathlib import Path

from state_guard import get_state, require_allowed

# -----------------------------
# PATHS
# -----------------------------
BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
DB_PATH = BASE_DIR / "data" / "memory.db"

# -----------------------------
# SANITY CHECKS
# -----------------------------
def sanity_check() -> None:
    # Treat sanity_check as an execution action (blocked in OBSERVE by STGRAIL)
    require_allowed("run_script")

    state = get_state()
    print(f"? Current STGRAIL state: {state}")

    if not DB_PATH.exists():
        raise FileNotFoundError("[ERROR] memory.db does not exist. Run init_db.py first (via recorder).")

    conn = sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH)
    cursor = conn.cursor()

    try:
        print("? Running sanity checks...\n")

        # 1) Check required tables exist (align to schema V1.1)
        required_tables = {
            "anchors",
            "chunks",
            "provenance_notes",
            "runs",
            "run_citations",
        }

        cursor.execute("SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table';")
        existing_tables = {row[0] for row in cursor.fetchall()}

        missing_tables = required_tables - existing_tables
        if missing_tables:
            raise RuntimeError(f"[ERROR] Missing tables: {sorted(missing_tables)}")

        print("[OK] All required tables present.")

        # 2) Anchors must exist before chunks (only illegal if chunks > 0 and anchors == 0)
        cursor.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM anchors;")
        anchor_count = cursor.fetchone()[0]

        cursor.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM chunks;")
        chunk_count = cursor.fetchone()[0]

        if chunk_count > 0 and anchor_count == 0:
            raise RuntimeError("[ERROR] Chunks exist but no anchors registered.")

        print(f"[OK] Anchors: {anchor_count}, Chunks: {chunk_count}")

        # 3) No NULL import_session_id in anchors or chunks
        cursor.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM anchors WHERE import_session_id IS NULL;")
        bad_anchors = cursor.fetchone()[0]

        cursor.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM chunks WHERE import_session_id IS NULL;")
        bad_chunks = cursor.fetchone()[0]

        if bad_anchors > 0 or bad_chunks > 0:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"[ERROR] Missing import_session_id (anchors: {bad_anchors}, chunks: {bad_chunks})"
            )

        print("[OK] import_session_id present on all anchors and chunks.")

        # 4) Lexicon chunks must have lexicon_word
        # Enforce ONLY for lexicon-located chunks (do not assume all definitions are lexicon)
        cursor.execute(
            """
            SELECT COUNT(*) FROM chunks
            WHERE anchor_locator LIKE 'lexicon:%'
              AND lexicon_word IS NULL;
            """
        )
        bad_lexicon = cursor.fetchone()[0]

        if bad_lexicon > 0:
            raise RuntimeError(f"[ERROR] {bad_lexicon} lexicon chunks missing lexicon_word.")

        print("[OK] Lexicon chunks correctly populated.")

        # 5) run_citations should never reference missing rows (basic FK sanity)
        # If foreign_keys is enabled, this should be clean?still a useful check.
        cursor.execute(
            """
            SELECT COUNT(*) FROM run_citations rc
            LEFT JOIN runs r ON r.run_id = rc.run_id
            LEFT JOIN chunks c ON c.chunk_id = rc.chunk_id
            WHERE r.run_id IS NULL OR c.chunk_id IS NULL;
            """
        )
        broken_edges = cursor.fetchone()[0]

        if broken_edges > 0:
            raise RuntimeError(f"[ERROR] run_citations has {broken_edges} broken edges (missing run or chunk).")

        print("[OK] run_citations edges are valid.")

        print("\n? SANITY CHECK PASSED ? DATABASE IS COHERENT.")

    finally:
        conn.close()


# -----------------------------
# SCRIPT ENTRY POINT
# -----------------------------
if __name__ == "__main__":
    sanity_check()
Why these edits are the ?most suitable? shape
No top-level side effects (no printing or state reads on import).

STGRAIL enforced even if someone runs the script directly (because it calls require_allowed("run_script")).

Required tables now match schema V1.1 (run_citations included).

Lexicon rule is precise: it checks anchor_locator LIKE 'lexicon:%', not truth_type='definition'.

Citation graph sanity is checked without adding new tables or behavior.

-- Normalized citation graph between runs and chunks

CREATE TABLE run_citations (
    run_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    chunk_id TEXT NOT NULL,

    PRIMARY KEY (run_id, chunk_id),

        REFERENCES runs(run_id)
        ON DELETE CASCADE,

        REFERENCES chunks(chunk_id)
        ON DELETE RESTRICT
);

You said:
ok scan : -- =========================================================
-- Solob Wrapper V1.1 ? Canonical SQLite Schema
-- Purpose: Anchor-referential, audit-first memory spine
-- Database role: Index + provenance ledger (NOT source of truth)
-- =========================================================

PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON;

-- =========================================================
-- TABLE: anchors
-- Indexes external, read-only anchor sources (PDF, JSON, text)
-- =========================================================
CREATE TABLE anchors (
    anchor_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    anchor_type TEXT NOT NULL,          -- lexicon | book | letter | other
    title TEXT NOT NULL,
    source_path TEXT NOT NULL,           -- filesystem path to anchor
    source_format TEXT NOT NULL,         -- json | pdf | txt
    status TEXT NOT NULL,                -- canon | working
    provenance TEXT NOT NULL,            -- human-entered source description
    import_session_id TEXT NOT NULL,     -- ingestion batch identifier
    created_at TEXT NOT NULL             -- ISO-8601 timestamp
);

-- =========================================================
-- TABLE: chunks
-- Atomic, referenceable statements extracted from anchors
-- =========================================================
CREATE TABLE chunks (
    chunk_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    anchor_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    anchor_locator TEXT NOT NULL,        -- page, section, lexicon row, etc.
    lexicon_word TEXT,                   -- headword if lexicon-derived (NULL otherwise)
    content TEXT NOT NULL,               -- exact extracted text (no mutation)

    truth_type TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (
        truth_type IN (
            'definition',
            'event',
            'empirical',
            'interpretive',
            'causal',
            'relational'
        )
    ),

    mutation_mode TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (
        mutation_mode IN (
            'append-only',
            'versioned',
            'time-bounded',
            're-index-only'
        )
    ),

    confidence REAL CHECK (
        confidence IS NULL
        OR (confidence >= 0 AND confidence <= 1)
    ),

    import_session_id TEXT NOT NULL,     -- ingestion batch identifier
    created_at TEXT NOT NULL,

    FOREIGN KEY (anchor_id)
        REFERENCES anchors(anchor_id)
        ON DELETE RESTRICT
);

-- =========================================================
-- TABLE: provenance_notes
-- Non-mutative annotations and corrections
-- =========================================================
CREATE TABLE provenance_notes (
    note_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    target_type TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (target_type IN ('anchor', 'chunk')),
    target_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    note TEXT NOT NULL,
    author TEXT NOT NULL,
    created_at TEXT NOT NULL
);

-- =========================================================
-- TABLE: runs
-- Logged system queries and outputs (audit trail)
-- =========================================================
CREATE TABLE runs (
    run_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    input_query TEXT NOT NULL,
    output_text TEXT NOT NULL,
    created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
    verdict TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (
        verdict IN ('ok', 'unknown', 'flagged')
    )
);

-- =========================================================
-- TABLE: run_citations
-- Normalized citation graph between runs and chunks
-- =========================================================
CREATE TABLE run_citations (
    run_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    chunk_id TEXT NOT NULL,

    PRIMARY KEY (run_id, chunk_id),

    FOREIGN KEY (run_id)
        REFERENCES runs(run_id)
        ON DELETE CASCADE,

    FOREIGN KEY (chunk_id)
        REFERENCES chunks(chunk_id)
        ON DELETE RESTRICT
);

-- =========================================================
-- INDEXES (AFTER TABLES EXIST)
-- =========================================================
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_chunks_anchor_id ON chunks(anchor_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_runs_created_at ON runs(created_at);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_prov_target ON provenance_notes(target_type, target_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_run_citations_chunk ON run_citations(chunk_id);

-- Optional but useful
-- CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_run_citations_run ON run_citations(run_id);

-- =========================================================
-- END OF SCHEMA ? V1.1

Based on a file-by-file static analysis of the repository, here is the verification of your system state:

1. Script Verification
scripts/run_recorded.py
: YES, it calls 
require_allowed("run_script")
 on line 20 inside the 
main()
 function. This ensures the "recorder" itself is subject to state control.
scripts/state_guard.py
: YES, it allows "run_script" in the RECORD state (as well as the EXECUTE state), as defined in the ALLOWED_ACTIONS dictionary on line 16.
scripts/init_db.py
: NO, it does not call

 directly. In fact, the file is currently effectively empty (containing only a source comment).
2. Guard Architecture: The "Door Level" Principle
init_db.py
 should not be listed in ALLOWED_ACTIONS in 
state_guard.py
.

The system is designed with a "Front Door" guard architecture. The guard is placed at the level of 
run_recorded.py
 (the "Run Script" door). Because 
init_db.py
 is intended to be executed through the recorder, the check happens before 
init_db.py
 is even loaded into a Python process.

Why this is superior: It centralizes the "gatekeeping" logic. You don't have to trust every individual script to "check their own ID" at the door; the recorder (
run_recorded.py
) ensures that if the system state is OBSERVE, the command to run 
init_db.py
 never even starts.

# scripts/run_recorded.py
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import os
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path

from hash_utils import sha256_file
from ops_log import log_event
from state_guard import require_allowed

BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
DATA_DIR = BASE_DIR / "data"
SCHEMA_PATH = DATA_DIR / "schema.sql"
DB_PATH = DATA_DIR / "memory.db"
LOGS_DIR = BASE_DIR / "logs"


def main() -> int:
    require_allowed("run_script")

    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--intent", required=True, help="Human intent for this run (required).")
    parser.add_argument("script_path", help="Path to the script to run.")
    parser.add_argument("script_args", nargs="*", help="Args passed to the script.")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    script_path = Path(args.script_path).resolve()
    if not script_path.exists():
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"Script not found: {script_path}")

    LOGS_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    schema_sha = sha256_file(SCHEMA_PATH) if SCHEMA_PATH.exists() else None
    db_sha_before = sha256_file(DB_PATH) if DB_PATH.exists() else None

    cmd = [sys.executable, str(script_path)] + list(args.script_args)

    # IMPORTANT: Mark child process as "recorded" so it can refuse unrecorded runs.
    env = os.environ.copy()
    env["SOLOB_RECORDED_RUN"] = "1"
    env["SOLOB_HUMAN_INTENT"] = args.intent

    proc = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, env=env)

    run_tag = script_path.stem
    out_path = LOGS_DIR / f"{run_tag}.stdout.log"
    err_path = LOGS_DIR / f"{run_tag}.stderr.log"
    out_path.write_text(proc.stdout, encoding="utf-8")
    err_path.write_text(proc.stderr, encoding="utf-8")

    db_sha_after = sha256_file(DB_PATH) if DB_PATH.exists() else None

    log_event(
        action="run_script",
        payload={"human_intent": args.intent, "cmd": cmd},
        artifacts={
            "exit_code": proc.returncode,
            "stdout_path": str(out_path.as_posix()),
            "stderr_path": str(err_path.as_posix()),
            "schema_sha256": schema_sha,
            "db_sha256_before": db_sha_before,
            "db_sha256_after": db_sha_after,
        },
    )

    print(proc.stdout, end="")
    if proc.stderr:
        print(proc.stderr, file=sys.stderr, end="")

    return proc.returncode


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())
WHY THIS MATTERS: NOW EVERY CHILD SCRIPT CAN KNOW: ?AM I BEING RUN THROUGH THE RECORDER??

# scripts/init_db.py
from __future__ import annotations

import os
import sqlite3
from pathlib import Path


BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
DATA_DIR = BASE_DIR / "data"

DB_PATH = DATA_DIR / "memory.db"
SCHEMA_PATH = DATA_DIR / "schema.sql"


def require_recorded_run() -> None:
    """
    HARD RULE: init_db MUST NOT RUN UNRECORDED.
    If you want to init the DB, run it through scripts/run_recorded.py.
    """
    if os.environ.get("SOLOB_RECORDED_RUN") != "1":
        raise RuntimeError(
            "BLOCKED: init_db.py must be executed via scripts/run_recorded.py "
            "so the action is logged (NO UNRECORDED SHUFFLES)."
        )


def init_db() -> None:
    require_recorded_run()

    if not SCHEMA_PATH.exists():
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"schema.sql not found at {SCHEMA_PATH}")

    DATA_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    conn = sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH)
    try:
        schema_sql = SCHEMA_PATH.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        conn.executescript(schema_sql)
        conn.commit()
        print("[OK] Database initialized successfully.")
        print(f"   DB: {DB_PATH}")
        print(f"   Schema: {SCHEMA_PATH}")
    finally:
        conn.close()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    init_db()

What?s actually happening
There are two different ?receipts? you can choose to generate:

A) snapshot_anchors.py already makes its own receipt
When you run snapshot_anchors.py, it:

writes the manifest file (the ?photo?)

calls log_event(action="snapshot_anchors", ...) -> writes a line to logs/ops_ledger.jsonl

So even without run_recorded.py, it still logs the action.

B) run_recorded.py is the ?cashier camera + CCTV? wrapper
When you run any script through run_recorded.py, it adds extra audit layers:

captures stdout/stderr into files

logs a run_script event with:

exit code

schema hash

DB hash before/after

intent

and it sets environment flags like SOLOB_RECORDED_RUN=1

So if you run snapshot_anchors.py through run_recorded.py, you?ll get two ledger lines:

action="run_script" (wrapper receipt)

action="snapshot_anchors" (script?s own receipt)

That?s not a bug. It?s just double-entry bookkeeping.

Think of it like:

run_recorded = the supermarket receipt + CCTV timestamp

snapshot_anchors = the delivery note inside the box

Both are valid. Together they?re overkill (in a good way) ? but you can choose.

[OK] Result: you get stdout/stderr logs for everything + two ledger lines for snapshot (run_script + snapshot_anchors).

BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
ANCHORS_DIR = BASE_DIR / "anchors"
SNAP_DIR = BASE_DIR / "data" / "snapshots"


def utc_now_iso() -> str:
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()


def snapshot_anchors() -> Path:
    # ? STGRAIL ENFORCEMENT
    require_allowed("snapshot_anchors")

    SNAP_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    ts = utc_now_iso().replace(":", "").replace("-", "")
    out_path = SNAP_DIR / f"anchors_manifest_{ts}.json"

    manifest = {
        "ts_utc": utc_now_iso(),
        "root": str(ANCHORS_DIR.as_posix()),
        "files": sha256_manifest(ANCHORS_DIR),
    }

    out_path.write_text(
        json.dumps(manifest, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, sort_keys=True),
        encoding="utf-8",
    )

    # ? OPS LEDGER ENTRY (AFTER ACTION PREP)
    log_event(
        action="snapshot_anchors",
        human_intent=(
            "Record a read-only cryptographic snapshot of all anchor files "
            "before any database initialization or ingestion."
        ),
        payload={"anchors_dir": str(ANCHORS_DIR.as_posix())},
        artifacts={
            "manifest_path": str(out_path.as_posix()),
            "file_count": len(manifest["files"]),
        },
    )

    return out_path


if __name__ == "__main__":
    p = snapshot_anchors()
    print(f"[OK] Anchor snapshot written: {p}")

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path

from hash_utils import sha256_manifest
from ops_log import log_event
from state_guard import require_allowed

BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
ANCHORS_DIR = BASE_DIR / "anchors"
SNAP_DIR = BASE_DIR / "data" / "snapshots"


def utc_now_iso() -> str:
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()


def safe_ts() -> str:
    # Windows-safe, filename-safe timestamp (no ":" characters)
    # Example: 20251220T034500Z
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ")


def snapshot_anchors() -> Path:
    # ? STGRAIL ENFORCEMENT
    require_allowed("snapshot_anchors")

    SNAP_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    out_path = SNAP_DIR / f"anchors_manifest_{safe_ts()}.json"

    manifest = {
        "ts_utc": utc_now_iso(),
        "root": str(ANCHORS_DIR.as_posix()),
        "files": sha256_manifest(ANCHORS_DIR),
    }

    out_path.write_text(
        json.dumps(manifest, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, sort_keys=True),
        encoding="utf-8",
    )

    # ? OPS LEDGER ENTRY (AFTER ACTION)
    log_event(
        action="snapshot_anchors",
        payload={
            "human_intent": (
                "Record a read-only cryptographic snapshot of all anchor files "
                "before any database initialization or ingestion."
            ),
            "anchors_dir": str(ANCHORS_DIR.as_posix()),
        },
        artifacts={
            "manifest_path": str(out_path.as_posix()),
            "file_count": len(manifest["files"]),
        },
    )

    return out_path


if __name__ == "__main__":
    p = snapshot_anchors()
    print(f"[OK] Anchor snapshot written: {p}")

# scripts/sanity_check.py
from __future__ import annotations

import sqlite3
from pathlib import Path

from state_guard import get_state, require_allowed

# -----------------------------
# PATHS
# -----------------------------
BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
DB_PATH = BASE_DIR / "data" / "memory.db"

# -----------------------------
# SANITY CHECKS
# -----------------------------
def sanity_check() -> None:
    # Treat sanity_check as an execution action (blocked in OBSERVE by STGRAIL)
    require_allowed("run_script")

    state = get_state()
    print(f"? Current STGRAIL state: {state}")

    if not DB_PATH.exists():
        raise FileNotFoundError("[ERROR] memory.db does not exist. Run init_db.py first (via recorder).")

    conn = sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH)
    cursor = conn.cursor()

    try:
        print("? Running sanity checks...\n")

        # 1) Check required tables exist (align to schema V1.1)
        required_tables = {
            "anchors",
            "chunks",
            "provenance_notes",
            "runs",
            "run_citations",
        }

        cursor.execute("SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table';")
        existing_tables = {row[0] for row in cursor.fetchall()}

        missing_tables = required_tables - existing_tables
        if missing_tables:
            raise RuntimeError(f"[ERROR] Missing tables: {sorted(missing_tables)}")

        print("[OK] All required tables present.")

        # 2) Anchors must exist before chunks (only illegal if chunks > 0 and anchors == 0)
        cursor.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM anchors;")
        anchor_count = cursor.fetchone()[0]

        cursor.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM chunks;")
        chunk_count = cursor.fetchone()[0]

        if chunk_count > 0 and anchor_count == 0:
            raise RuntimeError("[ERROR] Chunks exist but no anchors registered.")

        print(f"[OK] Anchors: {anchor_count}, Chunks: {chunk_count}")

        # 3) No NULL import_session_id in anchors or chunks
        cursor.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM anchors WHERE import_session_id IS NULL;")
        bad_anchors = cursor.fetchone()[0]

        cursor.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM chunks WHERE import_session_id IS NULL;")
        bad_chunks = cursor.fetchone()[0]

        if bad_anchors > 0 or bad_chunks > 0:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"[ERROR] Missing import_session_id (anchors: {bad_anchors}, chunks: {bad_chunks})"
            )

        print("[OK] import_session_id present on all anchors and chunks.")

        # 4) Lexicon chunks must have lexicon_word
        # Enforce ONLY for lexicon-located chunks (do not assume all definitions are lexicon)
        cursor.execute(
            """
            SELECT COUNT(*) FROM chunks
            WHERE anchor_locator LIKE 'lexicon:%'
              AND lexicon_word IS NULL;
            """
        )
        bad_lexicon = cursor.fetchone()[0]

        if bad_lexicon > 0:
            raise RuntimeError(f"[ERROR] {bad_lexicon} lexicon chunks missing lexicon_word.")

        print("[OK] Lexicon chunks correctly populated.")

        # 5) run_citations should never reference missing rows (basic FK sanity)
        # If foreign_keys is enabled, this should be clean?still a useful check.
        cursor.execute(
            """
            SELECT COUNT(*) FROM run_citations rc
            LEFT JOIN runs r ON r.run_id = rc.run_id
            LEFT JOIN chunks c ON c.chunk_id = rc.chunk_id
            WHERE r.run_id IS NULL OR c.chunk_id IS NULL;
            """
        )
        broken_edges = cursor.fetchone()[0]

        if broken_edges > 0:
            raise RuntimeError(f"[ERROR] run_citations has {broken_edges} broken edges (missing run or chunk).")

        print("[OK] run_citations edges are valid.")

        print("\n? SANITY CHECK PASSED ? DATABASE IS COHERENT.")

    finally:
        conn.close()


# -----------------------------
# SCRIPT ENTRY POINT
# -----------------------------
if __name__ == "__main__":
    sanity_check()

-- =========================================================
-- Solob Wrapper V1.1 ? Canonical SQLite Schema
-- Purpose: Anchor-referential, audit-first memory spine
-- Database role: Index + provenance ledger (NOT source of truth)
-- =========================================================

PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON;

-- =========================================================
-- TABLE: anchors
-- Indexes external, read-only anchor sources (PDF, JSON, text)
-- =========================================================
CREATE TABLE anchors (
    anchor_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    anchor_type TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (anchor_type IN ('lexicon', 'book', 'letter', 'other')),
    title TEXT NOT NULL,
    source_path TEXT NOT NULL,           -- filesystem path to anchor
    source_format TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (source_format IN ('json', 'pdf', 'txt')),
    status TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (status IN ('canon', 'working')),
    provenance TEXT NOT NULL,            -- human-entered source description
    import_session_id TEXT NOT NULL,     -- ingestion batch identifier
    created_at TEXT NOT NULL             -- ISO-8601 timestamp
);


-- =========================================================
-- TABLE: chunks
-- Atomic, referenceable statements extracted from anchors
-- =========================================================
CREATE TABLE chunks (
    chunk_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    anchor_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    anchor_locator TEXT NOT NULL,        -- page, section, lexicon row, etc.
    lexicon_word TEXT,                   -- headword if lexicon-derived (NULL otherwise)
    content TEXT NOT NULL,               -- exact extracted text (no mutation)

    truth_type TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (
        truth_type IN (
            'definition',
            'event',
            'empirical',
            'interpretive',
            'causal',
            'relational'
        )
    ),

    mutation_mode TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (
        mutation_mode IN (
            'append-only',
            'versioned',
            'time-bounded',
            're-index-only'
        )
    ),

    confidence REAL CHECK (
        confidence IS NULL
        OR (confidence >= 0 AND confidence <= 1)
    ),

    import_session_id TEXT NOT NULL,     -- ingestion batch identifier
    created_at TEXT NOT NULL,

    FOREIGN KEY (anchor_id)
        REFERENCES anchors(anchor_id)
        ON DELETE RESTRICT
);

-- =========================================================
-- TABLE: provenance_notes
-- Non-mutative annotations and corrections
-- =========================================================
CREATE TABLE provenance_notes (
    note_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    target_type TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (target_type IN ('anchor', 'chunk')),
    target_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    note TEXT NOT NULL,
    author TEXT NOT NULL,
    created_at TEXT NOT NULL
);

-- =========================================================
-- TABLE: runs
-- Logged system queries and outputs (audit trail)
-- =========================================================
CREATE TABLE runs (
    run_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    input_query TEXT NOT NULL,
    output_text TEXT NOT NULL,
    created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
    verdict TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (
        verdict IN ('ok', 'unknown', 'flagged')
    )
);

-- =========================================================
-- TABLE: run_citations
-- Normalized citation graph between runs and chunks
-- =========================================================
CREATE TABLE run_citations (
    run_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    chunk_id TEXT NOT NULL,

    PRIMARY KEY (run_id, chunk_id),

    FOREIGN KEY (run_id)
        REFERENCES runs(run_id)
        ON DELETE CASCADE,

    FOREIGN KEY (chunk_id)
        REFERENCES chunks(chunk_id)
        ON DELETE RESTRICT
);

-- =========================================================
-- INDEXES (AFTER TABLES EXIST)
-- =========================================================
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_chunks_anchor_id ON chunks(anchor_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_runs_created_at ON runs(created_at);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_prov_target ON provenance_notes(target_type, target_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_run_citations_chunk ON run_citations(chunk_id);

-- Optional but useful
-- CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_run_citations_run ON run_citations(run_id);

-- =========================================================
-- END OF SCHEMA ? V1.1
-- =========================================================

init_db.py:# scripts/init_db.py
from __future__ import annotations

import os
import sqlite3
from pathlib import Path


BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
DATA_DIR = BASE_DIR / "data"

DB_PATH = DATA_DIR / "memory.db"
SCHEMA_PATH = DATA_DIR / "schema.sql"


def require_recorded_run() -> None:
    """
    HARD RULE: init_db MUST NOT RUN UNRECORDED.
    If you want to init the DB, run it through scripts/run_recorded.py.
    """
    if os.environ.get("SOLOB_RECORDED_RUN") != "1":
        raise RuntimeError(
            "BLOCKED: init_db.py must be executed via scripts/run_recorded.py "
            "so the action is logged (NO UNRECORDED SHUFFLES)."
        )


def init_db() -> None:
    require_recorded_run()

    if not SCHEMA_PATH.exists():
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"schema.sql not found at {SCHEMA_PATH}")

    DATA_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    conn = sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH)
    try:
        schema_sql = SCHEMA_PATH.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        conn.executescript(schema_sql)
        conn.commit()
        print("[OK] Database initialized successfully.")
        print(f"   DB: {DB_PATH}")
        print(f"   Schema: {SCHEMA_PATH}")
    finally:
        conn.close()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    init_db()  // run_recorded.py: from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import os
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path

from hash_utils import sha256_file
from ops_log import log_event
from state_guard import require_allowed

BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
DATA_DIR = BASE_DIR / "data"
SCHEMA_PATH = DATA_DIR / "schema.sql"
DB_PATH = DATA_DIR / "memory.db"
LOGS_DIR = BASE_DIR / "logs"


def main() -> int:
    require_allowed("run_script")

    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--intent", required=True, help="Human intent for this run (required).")
    parser.add_argument("script_path", help="Path to the script to run.")
    parser.add_argument("script_args", nargs="*", help="Args passed to the script.")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    script_path = Path(args.script_path).resolve()
    if not script_path.exists():
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"Script not found: {script_path}")

    LOGS_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    schema_sha = sha256_file(SCHEMA_PATH) if SCHEMA_PATH.exists() else None
    db_sha_before = sha256_file(DB_PATH) if DB_PATH.exists() else None

    cmd = [sys.executable, str(script_path)] + list(args.script_args)

    # IMPORTANT: Mark child process as "recorded" so it can refuse unrecorded runs.
    env = os.environ.copy()
    env["SOLOB_RECORDED_RUN"] = "1"
    env["SOLOB_HUMAN_INTENT"] = args.intent

    proc = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, env=env)

    run_tag = script_path.stem
    out_path = LOGS_DIR / f"{run_tag}.stdout.log"
    err_path = LOGS_DIR / f"{run_tag}.stderr.log"
    out_path.write_text(proc.stdout, encoding="utf-8")
    err_path.write_text(proc.stderr, encoding="utf-8")

    db_sha_after = sha256_file(DB_PATH) if DB_PATH.exists() else None

    log_event(
        action="run_script",
        payload={"human_intent": args.intent, "cmd": cmd},
        artifacts={
            "exit_code": proc.returncode,
            "stdout_path": str(out_path.as_posix()),
            "stderr_path": str(err_path.as_posix()),
            "schema_sha256": schema_sha,
            "db_sha256_before": db_sha_before,
            "db_sha256_after": db_sha_after,
        },
    )

    print(proc.stdout, end="")
    if proc.stderr:
        print(proc.stderr, file=sys.stderr, end="")

    return proc.returncode


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

always log_event(...) even if preflight fails

# scripts/init_db.py
from __future__ import annotations

import os
import sqlite3
from pathlib import Path
print("? Intent:", os.environ.get("SOLOB_HUMAN_INTENT", "(none)"))


BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
DATA_DIR = BASE_DIR / "data"

DB_PATH = DATA_DIR / "memory.db"
SCHEMA_PATH = DATA_DIR / "schema.sql"


def require_recorded_run() -> None:
    """
    HARD RULE: init_db MUST NOT RUN UNRECORDED.
    If you want to init the DB, run it through scripts/run_recorded.py.
    """
    if os.environ.get("SOLOB_RECORDED_RUN") != "1":
        raise RuntimeError(
            "BLOCKED: init_db.py must be executed via scripts/run_recorded.py "
            "so the action is logged (NO UNRECORDED SHUFFLES)."
        )


def init_db() -> None:
    require_recorded_run()

    if not SCHEMA_PATH.exists():
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"schema.sql not found at {SCHEMA_PATH}")

    DATA_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    conn = sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH)
    conn.execute("PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON;")

    try:
        schema_sql = SCHEMA_PATH.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        conn.executescript(schema_sql)
        conn.commit()
        print("[OK] Database initialized successfully.")
        print(f"   DB: {DB_PATH}")
        print(f"   Schema: {SCHEMA_PATH}")
    finally:
        conn.close()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    init_db()

# scripts/init_db.py
from __future__ import annotations

import os
import sqlite3
from pathlib import Path

BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
DATA_DIR = BASE_DIR / "data"

DB_PATH = DATA_DIR / "memory.db"
SCHEMA_PATH = DATA_DIR / "schema.sql"


def require_recorded_run() -> None:
    """
    HARD RULE: init_db MUST NOT RUN UNRECORDED.
    If you want to init the DB, run it through scripts/run_recorded.py.
    """
    if os.environ.get("SOLOB_RECORDED_RUN") != "1":
        raise RuntimeError(
            "BLOCKED: init_db.py must be executed via scripts/run_recorded.py "
            "so the action is logged (NO UNRECORDED SHUFFLES)."
        )


def init_db() -> None:
    require_recorded_run()

    print("? Intent:", os.environ.get("SOLOB_HUMAN_INTENT", "(none)"))

    if not SCHEMA_PATH.exists():
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"schema.sql not found at {SCHEMA_PATH}")

    DATA_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    conn = sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH)
    try:
        conn.execute("PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON;")

        schema_sql = SCHEMA_PATH.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        conn.executescript(schema_sql)
        conn.commit()

        print("[OK] Database initialized successfully.")
        print(f"   DB: {DB_PATH}")
        print(f"   Schema: {SCHEMA_PATH}")
    finally:
        conn.close()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    init_db()

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
import platform
import sys
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Dict, Optional

BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
LOGS_DIR = BASE_DIR / "logs"
LEDGER_PATH = LOGS_DIR / "ops_ledger.jsonl"


def utc_now_iso() -> str:
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()


def append_jsonl(path: Path, obj: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with path.open("a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        f.write(json.dumps(obj, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True) + "\n")


def log_event(
    action: str,
    *,
    human_intent: str,
    payload: Dict[str, Any],
    artifacts: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,
) -> None:
    """
    Records an irreversible operation with explicit human intent.
    """
    evt = {
        "ts_utc": utc_now_iso(),
        "action": action,
        "human_intent": human_intent,
        "payload": payload,
        "artifacts": artifacts or {},
        "env": {
            "python": sys.version.split()[0],
            "platform": platform.platform(),
            "cwd": str(Path.cwd()),
            "user": os.environ.get("USERNAME") or os.environ.get("USER") or "unknown",
        },
    }
    append_jsonl(LEDGER_PATH, evt) //// hash_utils.py:from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
from pathlib import Path
from typing import List, Tuple


def sha256_file(path: Path, chunk_size: int = 1024 * 1024) -> str:
    h = hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open("rb") as f:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: f.read(chunk_size), b""):
            h.update(chunk)
    return h.hexdigest()


def list_files(root: Path) -> List[Path]:
    # Deterministic ordering
    return sorted([p for p in root.rglob("*") if p.is_file()])


def sha256_manifest(root: Path) -> List[Tuple[str, str]]:
    """
    Returns list of (relative_path, sha256) for every file under root, sorted.
    """
    files = list_files(root)
    out: List[Tuple[str, str]] = []
    for f in files:
        rel = f.relative_to(root).as_posix()
        out.append((rel, sha256_file(f)))
    return out

2) FINAL hash_utils.py (copy/paste as ONE FILE)
[DIR] scripts/hash_utils.py

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
from pathlib import Path
from typing import List, Tuple


def sha256_file(path: Path, chunk_size: int = 1024 * 1024) -> str:
    h = hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open("rb") as f:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: f.read(chunk_size), b""):
            h.update(chunk)
    return h.hexdigest()


def list_files(root: Path) -> List[Path]:
    # Deterministic ordering
    return sorted([p for p in root.rglob("*") if p.is_file()])


def sha256_manifest(root: Path) -> List[Tuple[str, str]]:
    """
    Returns list of (relative_path, sha256) for every file under root, sorted.
    """
    files = list_files(root)
    out: List[Tuple[str, str]] = []
    for f in files:
        rel = f.relative_to(root).as_posix()
        out.append((rel, sha256_file(f)))
    return out
[OK] Good: stable ordering, stable relative paths, stable hashing.

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import os
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path

from hash_utils import sha256_file
from ops_log import log_event
from state_guard import require_allowed

BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
DATA_DIR = BASE_DIR / "data"
SCHEMA_PATH = DATA_DIR / "schema.sql"
DB_PATH = DATA_DIR / "memory.db"
LOGS_DIR = BASE_DIR / "logs"


def main() -> int:
    require_allowed("run_script")

    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--intent", required=True, help="Human intent for this run (required).")
    parser.add_argument("script_path", help="Path to the script to run.")
    parser.add_argument("script_args", nargs="*", help="Args passed to the script.")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    script_path = Path(args.script_path).resolve()
    if not script_path.exists():
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"Script not found: {script_path}")

    LOGS_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    schema_sha = sha256_file(SCHEMA_PATH) if SCHEMA_PATH.exists() else None
    db_sha_before = sha256_file(DB_PATH) if DB_PATH.exists() else None

    cmd = [sys.executable, str(script_path)] + list(args.script_args)

    # IMPORTANT: Mark child process as "recorded" so it can refuse unrecorded runs.
    env = os.environ.copy()
    env["SOLOB_RECORDED_RUN"] = "1"
    env["SOLOB_HUMAN_INTENT"] = args.intent

    proc = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, env=env)

    run_tag = script_path.stem
    out_path = LOGS_DIR / f"{run_tag}.stdout.log"
    err_path = LOGS_DIR / f"{run_tag}.stderr.log"
    out_path.write_text(proc.stdout, encoding="utf-8")
    err_path.write_text(proc.stderr, encoding="utf-8")

    db_sha_after = sha256_file(DB_PATH) if DB_PATH.exists() else None

log_event(
    action="run_script",
    human_intent=args.intent,
    payload={"cmd": cmd},
    artifacts={
        "exit_code": proc.returncode,
        "stdout_path": str(out_path.as_posix()),
        "stderr_path": str(err_path.as_posix()),
        "schema_sha256": schema_sha,
        "db_sha256_before": db_sha_before,
        "db_sha256_after": db_sha_after,
    },
)

    print(proc.stdout, end="")
    if proc.stderr:
        print(proc.stderr, file=sys.stderr, end="")

    return proc.returncode


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import os
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path

from hash_utils import sha256_file
from ops_log import log_event
from state_guard import require_allowed

BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
DATA_DIR = BASE_DIR / "data"
SCHEMA_PATH = DATA_DIR / "schema.sql"
DB_PATH = DATA_DIR / "memory.db"
LOGS_DIR = BASE_DIR / "logs"


def main() -> int:
    require_allowed("run_script")

    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--intent", required=True, help="Human intent for this run (required).")
    parser.add_argument("script_path", help="Path to the script to run.")
    parser.add_argument("script_args", nargs="*", help="Args passed to the script.")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    script_path = Path(args.script_path).resolve()
    if not script_path.exists():
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"Script not found: {script_path}")

    LOGS_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    schema_sha = sha256_file(SCHEMA_PATH) if SCHEMA_PATH.exists() else None
    db_sha_before = sha256_file(DB_PATH) if DB_PATH.exists() else None

    cmd = [sys.executable, str(script_path)] + list(args.script_args)

    # IMPORTANT: Mark child process as "recorded" so it can refuse unrecorded runs.
    env = os.environ.copy()
    env["SOLOB_RECORDED_RUN"] = "1"
    env["SOLOB_HUMAN_INTENT"] = args.intent

    proc = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, env=env)

    run_tag = script_path.stem
    out_path = LOGS_DIR / f"{run_tag}.stdout.log"
    err_path = LOGS_DIR / f"{run_tag}.stderr.log"
    out_path.write_text(proc.stdout, encoding="utf-8")
    err_path.write_text(proc.stderr, encoding="utf-8")

    db_sha_after = sha256_file(DB_PATH) if DB_PATH.exists() else None

    # [OK] Ledger entry (must be inside main)
    log_event(
        action="run_script",
        human_intent=args.intent,
        payload={"cmd": cmd},
        artifacts={
            "exit_code": proc.returncode,
            "stdout_path": str(out_path.as_posix()),
            "stderr_path": str(err_path.as_posix()),
            "schema_sha256": schema_sha,
            "db_sha256_before": db_sha_before,
            "db_sha256_after": db_sha_after,
        },
    )

    print(proc.stdout, end="")
    if proc.stderr:
        print(proc.stderr, file=sys.stderr, end="")

    return proc.returncode


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path

from hash_utils import sha256_manifest

from state_guard import require_allowed

def utc_now_iso() -> str:
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()


def safe_ts() -> str:
    # Windows-safe, filename-safe timestamp (no ":" characters)
    # Example: 20251220T034500Z
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ")


def snapshot_anchors() -> Path:
    # ? STGRAIL ENFORCEMENT
    require_allowed("snapshot_anchors")

        "ts_utc": utc_now_iso(),
        "root": str(ANCHORS_DIR.as_posix()),
        "files": sha256_manifest(ANCHORS_DIR),
    }

        json.dumps(manifest, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, sort_keys=True),
        encoding="utf-8",
    )

        },
    )

    return out_path

[DIR] FILE: scripts/snapshot_anchors.py

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path

from hash_utils import sha256_manifest
from ops_log import log_event
from state_guard import require_allowed

BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
ANCHORS_DIR = BASE_DIR / "anchors"
SNAP_DIR = BASE_DIR / "data" / "snapshots"


def utc_now_iso() -> str:
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()


def safe_ts() -> str:
    # Windows-safe, filename-safe timestamp (no ":" characters)
    # Example: 20251220T034500Z
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ")


def require_recorded_run() -> None:
    """
    HARD RULE: snapshot MUST NOT RUN UNRECORDED.
    Run it via scripts/run_recorded.py so the action is logged.
    """
    if os.environ.get("SOLOB_RECORDED_RUN") != "1":
        raise RuntimeError(
            "BLOCKED: snapshot_anchors.py must be executed via scripts/run_recorded.py "
            "so the action is logged (NO UNRECORDED SHUFFLES)."
        )


def snapshot_anchors() -> Path:
    # ? Must be recorded + allowed by state
    require_recorded_run()
    require_allowed("snapshot_anchors")

    if not ANCHORS_DIR.exists():
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"anchors/ folder not found at: {ANCHORS_DIR}")

    SNAP_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    out_path = SNAP_DIR / f"anchors_manifest_{safe_ts()}.json"

    manifest = {
        "ts_utc": utc_now_iso(),
        "root": str(ANCHORS_DIR.as_posix()),
        "files": sha256_manifest(ANCHORS_DIR),
    }

    out_path.write_text(
        json.dumps(manifest, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, sort_keys=True),
        encoding="utf-8",
    )

    # ? OPS LEDGER ENTRY (AFTER ACTION)
    intent = os.environ.get(
        "SOLOB_HUMAN_INTENT",
        "Record a read-only cryptographic snapshot of all anchor files before any DB initialization or ingestion.",
    )

    log_event(
        action="snapshot_anchors",
        human_intent=intent,
        payload={"anchors_dir": str(ANCHORS_DIR.as_posix())},
        artifacts={
            "manifest_path": str(out_path.as_posix()),
            "file_count": len(manifest["files"]),
        },
    )

    return out_path


if __name__ == "__main__":
    p = snapshot_anchors()
    print(f"? Intent: {os.environ.get('SOLOB_HUMAN_INTENT', '(none)')}")
    print(f"[OK] Anchor snapshot written: {p}")

Can?t run directly (must be via run_recorded.py) -> no stealth ?shuffle?

State-gated (require_allowed("snapshot_anchors"))

Ledger call matches your ops_log.py (human_intent= is top-level)

Uses SOLOB_HUMAN_INTENT automatically so the intent in the recorder and the intent in the action can stay aligned

    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()


def safe_ts() -> str:
    # Windows-safe, filename-safe timestamp (no ":" characters)
    # Example: 20251220T034500Z
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ")


def require_recorded_run() -> None:
    """
    HARD RULE: snapshot MUST NOT RUN UNRECORDED.
    Run it via scripts/run_recorded.py so the action is logged.
    """
    if os.environ.get("SOLOB_RECORDED_RUN") != "1":
        raise RuntimeError(
            "BLOCKED: snapshot_anchors.py must be executed via scripts/run_recorded.py "
            "so the action is logged (NO UNRECORDED SHUFFLES)."
        )


def snapshot_anchors() -> Path:
    # ? Must be recorded + allowed by state
    require_recorded_run()
    require_allowed("snapshot_anchors")

    if not ANCHORS_DIR.exists():
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"anchors/ folder not found at: {ANCHORS_DIR}")

    SNAP_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    out_path = SNAP_DIR / f"anchors_manifest_{safe_ts()}.json"

    manifest = {
        "ts_utc": utc_now_iso(),
        "root": str(ANCHORS_DIR.as_posix()),
        "files": sha256_manifest(ANCHORS_DIR),
    }

    out_path.write_text(
        json.dumps(manifest, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, sort_keys=True),
        encoding="utf-8",
    )

    # ? OPS LEDGER ENTRY (AFTER ACTION)
    intent = os.environ.get(
        "SOLOB_HUMAN_INTENT",
        "Record a read-only cryptographic snapshot of all anchor files before any DB initialization or ingestion.",
    )

    log_event(
        action="snapshot_anchors",
        human_intent=intent,
        payload={"anchors_dir": str(ANCHORS_DIR.as_posix())},
        artifacts={
            "manifest_path": str(out_path.as_posix()),
            "file_count": len(manifest["files"]),
        },
    )

    return out_path


if __name__ == "__main__":
    p = snapshot_anchors()
    print(f"? Intent: {os.environ.get('SOLOB_HUMAN_INTENT', '(none)')}")
    print(f"[OK] Anchor snapshot written: {p}") ////

1) FIX scripts/ops_log.py (FULL FILE REPLACE)
File: solob-wrapper/scripts/ops_log.py

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
import platform
import sys
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Dict, Optional

BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
LOGS_DIR = BASE_DIR / "logs"
LEDGER_PATH = LOGS_DIR / "ops_ledger.jsonl"


def utc_now_iso() -> str:
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()


def append_jsonl(path: Path, obj: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with path.open("a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        f.write(json.dumps(obj, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True) + "\n")


def log_event(
    action: str,
    *,
    human_intent: str,
    payload: Dict[str, Any],
    artifacts: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,
) -> None:
    """
    Canonical ops ledger write. Intent is mandatory.
    """
    evt = {
        "ts_utc": utc_now_iso(),
        "action": action,
        "human_intent": human_intent,
        "payload": payload,
        "artifacts": artifacts or {},
        "env": {
            "python": sys.version.split()[0],
            "platform": platform.platform(),
            "cwd": str(Path.cwd()),
            "user": os.environ.get("USERNAME") or os.environ.get("USER") or "unknown",
        },
    }
    append_jsonl(LEDGER_PATH, evt)
2) FIX scripts/hash_utils.py (FULL FILE REPLACE)
File: solob-wrapper/scripts/hash_utils.py

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
from pathlib import Path
from typing import List, Tuple


def sha256_file(path: Path, chunk_size: int = 1024 * 1024) -> str:
    h = hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open("rb") as f:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: f.read(chunk_size), b""):
            h.update(chunk)
    return h.hexdigest()


def list_files(root: Path) -> List[Path]:
    return sorted([p for p in root.rglob("*") if p.is_file()])


def sha256_manifest(root: Path) -> List[Tuple[str, str]]:
    """
    Returns list of (relative_path, sha256) for every file under root, sorted.
    """
    out: List[Tuple[str, str]] = []
    for f in list_files(root):
        rel = f.relative_to(root).as_posix()
        out.append((rel, sha256_file(f)))
    return out
3) FIX scripts/state_guard.py (REMOVE SELF-IMPORT BUG)
You showed you have this at top:

from state_guard import get_state
That line must go. It?s the snake eating itself.

File: solob-wrapper/scripts/state_guard.py

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Literal

BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
STATE_PATH = BASE_DIR / "docs" / "STATE.json"

State = Literal["OBSERVE", "RECORD", "EXECUTE"]

ALLOWED_ACTIONS = {
    "OBSERVE": set(),
    "RECORD": {"snapshot_anchors", "run_script"},
    "EXECUTE": {"snapshot_anchors", "run_script"},
}


def get_state() -> State:
    if not STATE_PATH.exists():
        raise RuntimeError(f"STATE is undeclared. Missing: {STATE_PATH}")

    data = json.loads(STATE_PATH.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    state = data.get("state")
    if state not in ("OBSERVE", "RECORD", "EXECUTE"):
        raise RuntimeError(f"Invalid state in STATE.json: {state}")
    return state  # type: ignore[return-value]


def require_allowed(action: str) -> None:
    state = get_state()
    allowed = ALLOWED_ACTIONS.get(state, set())
    if action not in allowed:
        raise RuntimeError(
            f"Blocked by STGRAIL. Current state={state}. Action='{action}' not allowed."
        )
4) FIX scripts/run_recorded.py (INDENT + UTF8 + STOP PRINTING RAW OUTPUT)
Your pasted version had log_event(...) outside the function block (indent broke).
Also, Windows console encoding is why you got the Unicode error. The clean fix is: don?t print child output; always write logs.

File: solob-wrapper/scripts/run_recorded.py

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import os
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path

from hash_utils import sha256_file
from ops_log import log_event
from state_guard import require_allowed

BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
DATA_DIR = BASE_DIR / "data"
SCHEMA_PATH = DATA_DIR / "schema.sql"
DB_PATH = DATA_DIR / "memory.db"
LOGS_DIR = BASE_DIR / "logs"


def main() -> int:
    require_allowed("run_script")

    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--intent", required=True, help="Human intent for this run (required).")
    parser.add_argument("script_path", help="Path to the script to run.")
    parser.add_argument("script_args", nargs="*", help="Args passed to the script.")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    script_path = Path(args.script_path).resolve()
    if not script_path.exists():
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"Script not found: {script_path}")

    LOGS_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    schema_sha = sha256_file(SCHEMA_PATH) if SCHEMA_PATH.exists() else None
    db_sha_before = sha256_file(DB_PATH) if DB_PATH.exists() else None

    cmd = [sys.executable, str(script_path)] + list(args.script_args)

    env = os.environ.copy()
    env["SOLOB_RECORDED_RUN"] = "1"
    env["SOLOB_HUMAN_INTENT"] = args.intent

    # Force UTF-8 behavior inside child processes (helps Windows)
    env["PYTHONUTF8"] = "1"
    env["PYTHONIOENCODING"] = "utf-8"

    proc = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, env=env)

    run_tag = script_path.stem
    out_path = LOGS_DIR / f"{run_tag}.stdout.log"
    err_path = LOGS_DIR / f"{run_tag}.stderr.log"
    out_path.write_text(proc.stdout or "", encoding="utf-8")
    err_path.write_text(proc.stderr or "", encoding="utf-8")

    db_sha_after = sha256_file(DB_PATH) if DB_PATH.exists() else None

    log_event(
        action="run_script",
        human_intent=args.intent,
        payload={"cmd": cmd},
        artifacts={
            "exit_code": proc.returncode,
            "stdout_path": str(out_path.as_posix()),
            "stderr_path": str(err_path.as_posix()),
            "schema_sha256": schema_sha,
            "db_sha256_before": db_sha_before,
            "db_sha256_after": db_sha_after,
        },
    )

    # No raw printing of stdout/stderr to avoid Windows UnicodeEncodeError.
    print(f"[RECORDED] exit_code={proc.returncode} | stdout={out_path} | stderr={err_path}")
    return proc.returncode


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())
5) FIX scripts/init_db.py (YOUR VERSION WAS TRUNCATED)
File: solob-wrapper/scripts/init_db.py

from __future__ import annotations

import os
import sqlite3
from pathlib import Path

BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
DATA_DIR = BASE_DIR / "data"

DB_PATH = DATA_DIR / "memory.db"
SCHEMA_PATH = DATA_DIR / "schema.sql"


def require_recorded_run() -> None:
    """
    HARD RULE: init_db MUST NOT RUN UNRECORDED.
    Run it via scripts/run_recorded.py so the action is logged.
    """
    if os.environ.get("SOLOB_RECORDED_RUN") != "1":
        raise RuntimeError(
            "BLOCKED: init_db.py must be executed via scripts/run_recorded.py "
            "(NO UNRECORDED SHUFFLES)."
        )


def init_db() -> None:
    require_recorded_run()

    if not SCHEMA_PATH.exists():
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"schema.sql not found at {SCHEMA_PATH}")

    DATA_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    print("Intent:", os.environ.get("SOLOB_HUMAN_INTENT", "(none)"))

    conn = sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH)
    try:
        conn.execute("PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON;")
        schema_sql = SCHEMA_PATH.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        conn.executescript(schema_sql)
        conn.commit()
        print("Database initialized successfully.")
        print(f"DB: {DB_PATH}")
        print(f"Schema: {SCHEMA_PATH}")
    finally:
        conn.close()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    init_db()

File: solob-wrapper/scripts/snapshot_anchors.py

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path

from hash_utils import sha256_manifest
from ops_log import log_event
from state_guard import require_allowed

BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
ANCHORS_DIR = BASE_DIR / "anchors"
SNAP_DIR = BASE_DIR / "data" / "snapshots"


def utc_now_iso() -> str:
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()


def safe_ts() -> str:
    # Windows-safe filename timestamp
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ")


def require_recorded_run() -> None:
    """
    HARD RULE: snapshot MUST NOT RUN UNRECORDED.
    Run it via scripts/run_recorded.py so the action is logged.
    """
    if os.environ.get("SOLOB_RECORDED_RUN") != "1":
        raise RuntimeError(
            "BLOCKED: snapshot_anchors.py must be executed via scripts/run_recorded.py "
            "(NO UNRECORDED SHUFFLES)."
        )


def snapshot_anchors() -> Path:
    require_recorded_run()
    require_allowed("snapshot_anchors")

    if not ANCHORS_DIR.exists():
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"anchors/ folder not found at: {ANCHORS_DIR}")

    SNAP_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    out_path = SNAP_DIR / f"anchors_manifest_{safe_ts()}.json"

    manifest = {
        "ts_utc": utc_now_iso(),
        "root": str(ANCHORS_DIR.as_posix()),
        "files": sha256_manifest(ANCHORS_DIR),
    }

    out_path.write_text(
        json.dumps(manifest, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, sort_keys=True),
        encoding="utf-8",
    )

    intent = os.environ.get(
        "SOLOB_HUMAN_INTENT",
        "Record a read-only cryptographic snapshot of all anchor files before any DB initialization or ingestion.",
    )

    log_event(
        action="snapshot_anchors",
        human_intent=intent,
        payload={"anchors_dir": str(ANCHORS_DIR.as_posix())},
        artifacts={
            "manifest_path": str(out_path.as_posix()),
            "file_count": len(manifest["files"]),
        },
    )

    return out_path


if __name__ == "__main__":
    p = snapshot_anchors()
    print(f"Intent: {os.environ.get('SOLOB_HUMAN_INTENT', '(none)')}")
    print(f"Anchor snapshot written: {p}")

File: solob-wrapper/scripts/sanity_check.py

from __future__ import annotations

import sqlite3
from pathlib import Path

from state_guard import get_state, require_allowed

BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
DB_PATH = BASE_DIR / "data" / "memory.db"


def sanity_check() -> None:
    require_allowed("run_script")

    state = get_state()
    print(f"Current STGRAIL state: {state}")

    if not DB_PATH.exists():
        raise FileNotFoundError("memory.db does not exist. Run init_db.py first via recorder.")

    conn = sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH)
    cursor = conn.cursor()

    try:
        print("Running sanity checks...")

        required_tables = {
            "anchors",
            "chunks",
            "provenance_notes",
            "runs",
            "run_citations",
        }

        cursor.execute("SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table';")
        existing_tables = {row[0] for row in cursor.fetchall()}

        missing_tables = required_tables - existing_tables
        if missing_tables:
            raise RuntimeError(f"Missing tables: {sorted(missing_tables)}")

        print("All required tables present.")

        cursor.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM anchors;")
        anchor_count = cursor.fetchone()[0]

        cursor.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM chunks;")
        chunk_count = cursor.fetchone()[0]

        if chunk_count > 0 and anchor_count == 0:
            raise RuntimeError("Chunks exist but no anchors registered.")

        print(f"Anchors: {anchor_count}, Chunks: {chunk_count}")

        cursor.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM anchors WHERE import_session_id IS NULL;")
        bad_anchors = cursor.fetchone()[0]

        cursor.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM chunks WHERE import_session_id IS NULL;")
        bad_chunks = cursor.fetchone()[0]

        if bad_anchors > 0 or bad_chunks > 0:
            raise RuntimeError(f"Missing import_session_id (anchors: {bad_anchors}, chunks: {bad_chunks})")

        print("import_session_id present on all anchors and chunks.")

        cursor.execute(
            """
            SELECT COUNT(*) FROM chunks
            WHERE anchor_locator LIKE 'lexicon:%'
              AND lexicon_word IS NULL;
            """
        )
        bad_lexicon = cursor.fetchone()[0]
        if bad_lexicon > 0:
            raise RuntimeError(f"{bad_lexicon} lexicon chunks missing lexicon_word.")

        print("Lexicon chunks correctly populated.")

        cursor.execute(
            """
            SELECT COUNT(*) FROM run_citations rc
            LEFT JOIN runs r ON r.run_id = rc.run_id
            LEFT JOIN chunks c ON c.chunk_id = rc.chunk_id
            WHERE r.run_id IS NULL OR c.chunk_id IS NULL;
            """
        )
        broken_edges = cursor.fetchone()[0]
        if broken_edges > 0:
            raise RuntimeError(f"run_citations has {broken_edges} broken edges.")

        print("run_citations edges are valid.")
        print("SANITY CHECK PASSED - DATABASE IS COHERENT.")

    finally:
        conn.close()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sanity_check()
SCHEMA NOTE (YOUR QUESTION ABOUT target_type CHECK)
[OK] This part is correct inside the CREATE TABLE provenance_notes (...) block:

target_type TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (target_type IN ('anchor', 'chunk')),
But you cannot do your earlier ?subquery check? inside SQLite CHECK constraints like:

CHECK (target_id IN (SELECT anchor_id FROM anchors))
SQLite doesn?t allow that kind of cross-table subquery check in CHECK. If you want strict enforcement later, we do it with TRIGGERS (optional V1.2).

So: keep the target_type IN ('anchor','chunk') check exactly where you have it now (inside the table columns), and do not add cross-table checks in CHECK.
